## 11. THE NEWMAN CENTER CAST (St. Brendan the Navigator)

- **Fr. Gabriel Osei** (above).
- **Mrs. Dolores Fitch, 71.** Parish secretary since 1979. Widow. Lemon bars. Remembers every student by name and every
  sin by reputation. A gossip with a heart of gold leaf. Knew Nora; keeps her choir folder. *"You'll sing? We lost our
  best soprano last spring."* Source.
- **Veritas Missionaries** (a fictional national campus-ministry corps, two-year stints):
  - **Tyler Brandt, 26.** Cincinnati (St. Xavier HS — Jack's Ohio rival, Bengals vs. Browns, Moeller vs. Mooney in his
    head), former Miami (Ohio) linebacker. Relentless "discipleship" (wants to meet you for coffee *every week*). Genuinely
    holy and genuinely exhausting. Fist-bumps after Mass. *"Brother. Brother. Let's grab a coffee and talk about your
    prayer life."* Arc: in S4 he's the one who sits with Theo in county lockup for six hours.
  - **Bridget McNulty, 24.** Franciscan University of Steubenville '07. Soft-spoken, the sharpest theological mind in the
    building, secretly in a dark night of the soul she tells no one about. Lena's ally. S2: she leaves the missionaries
    mid-year; Lena is furious, then understands.
- **Earnest kids:**
  - **Joey Abruzzo**, freshman, Staten Island, b. Jan 17, 1991. Loud, joyful, wants to be a priest, says so constantly,
    is extremely interested in girls, says *that* constantly. Comic relief and a real vocation; he enters seminary in
    2013. Lives in Cabrillo Hall. *"Jack! JACK. You're Italian? Half? Half counts, the good half."*
  - **Becca Lindell** (above).
- **The rival couple — Peter Haddad and Kenzie Rourke.** Juniors. Peter (b. Mar 8, 1989): Orange County Maronite family,
  political science, Newman president, perfect, ambitious (wants to be a congressman), lector. Kenzie (b. Oct 1, 1989):
  Sacramento, nursing, cantor with a gorgeous voice, kind. Newman's "it couple"; they give the Theology of the Body talk
  every fall ("Love Is Patient: Our Chastity Story"). 🔒 **They are not chaste and haven't been since the summer of 2008.**
  Peter is terrified of being found out; Kenzie is tired of lying. Kenzie gets pregnant in S3; Peter's first instinct is
  the clinic, and Kenzie's is the opposite, and the Newman community gets a real-life test of everything it says. They
  marry (S3), divorce (adult seasons). They are the dark mirror of Jack and Lena: a couple whose performance of holiness
  is the problem, not their sin. Not villains: Peter's hypocrisy is fear; his eventual honesty is brave.
  **(E06 plan — the talk, Fri Sep 18, 7:30 p.m., Newman House lounge:)** a PowerPoint on a borrowed projector; 1 Cor 13:4
  on the title slide; photos (the Disneyland one; Kenzie's cousin's wedding); their slideshow song is Jason Mraz & Colbie
  Caillat, "Lucky"; Tyler MCs. They met at a Steubenville youth conference (summer 2007), "chose to wait" (🔒). At the Q&A
  **Margot reads Eph 5:22–24 (RSV) and asks Kenzie whether she plans to submit.** **Kenzie** answers with v. 21 and v. 25 —
  she'll submit to a man exactly as far as he's already laying his life down for her, and she'll know because he'll have been
  doing it every day before she's asked — better than Margot expected; Margot says so. **Peter**: "Practically? When we can't
  agree, I make the call. Prayerfully." Kenzie's face. If Jack asks *"What do you do when you fall?"*, Peter: **"By God's
  grace, we haven't had to find out."** Kenzie looks at her hands (🔒 the lie; she is tired of it). At the BBQ Kenzie tells
  Jack, quietly, "Good question. Nobody asks that one." Peter avoids him.
- **The choir:** led by **Gus Ferreira**, 60s, retired Portuguese-American tuna-fleet captain, organist, a voice like a
  foghorn.
- **The 9 p.m. Sunday Mass** is the college Mass: candles, guitar, standing room. The 11:30 is the "families and
  serious people" Mass; the pilot's collision happens there.

## 12. OTHER RECURRING PLAYERS

- **Doug Pruitt** (b. Aug 3, 1991), Kevin's roommate, Bakersfield, mechanical engineering, World of Warcraft (a level-80
  Tauren druid), sweet, oblivious, says "dude" with five vowels. 🔒 He is the 3 a.m. fire-alarm puller — a sleepwalker.
  Revealed S1E20.
- **Sloane Kittredge** (b. Dec 2, 1987), senior, Pi Lambda Chi rush chair, Laney's future "big," blonde, glossy, cool.
  🔒 Nora's sophomore roommate (Serra Hall 3B). She was at the Lighthouse on April 25 and saw Nora leave the first time.
  On the pilot night she leaves the cove bonfire with Theo (if Jack is there to see it) — neither of them knows what the
  other has to do with that night.
- **Det. Sgt. Ray Ochoa**, 49, Marisol PD, Rafi's father. Decent, tired, Catholic (St. Brendan's 9:30, back row). Closed
  the Calloway case as accidental — partly out of evidence, partly out of mercy to a family he privately believed had lost
  a daughter to suicide. 🔒 He knew about the pregnancy from the autopsy and kept it out of the public record at the
  Calloways' request. **(E06 plan — on-page debut:)** a gray unmarked Crown Victoria with a dash light; driving home at 1:30
  a.m. on Ocean Road, he recognizes his son's skateboard towing a kid on a bike with no light, lights them up, and does the
  bit: **"License and registration."** Dry, Westside, tired, funny in a dad's register; Spanish with Rafi; knows Jack as
  "Gloria's dish man — Rudy's kitchen" (he and Rudy nod at the 9:30 and don't talk). Asks his son what's in the bag; Rafi:
  "Vitamins, Pop"; **Ray does not open it** (🔒 the same mercy that closed the Calloway case — he chooses not to look; never
  juxtaposed with anything Nora). Gives Jack a clip-on bike light out of his trunk ("found property") and a line about
  Ocean Road: "I've picked enough kids up off this road." Jack learns: Rafi's father is a Marisol PD detective named Ray.
  **Lena knows the name Ochoa** (🔒 he took her statement April 26): in the E06 two-hander, when Jack says it, she touches the
  medal and changes the subject. One touch; no gloss; never again in S1 before E13.
  **(E12 plan:)** Fri Oct 30 at the Anchor counter, coffee to go, Halloween in a windbreaker: Marisol PD has everybody on Del Playa and Seacliff
  tomorrow; "**Stay off the bluff edges, Saint Isidore. Every Halloween somebody goes off one in Las Olas, and I'm the one who calls the mother.**"
  (🔒 never juxtaposed with Nora.) Sat Oct 31, from a unit on Del Playa, on the bullhorn, to Pope Joan on the widow's walk: "**Your Holiness. Please
  come down off the roof.**" Jack: "That's Rafi's dad."
- **Brooks Callahan** (b. July 4, 1988), senior, Sigma Rho, Newport Beach, lacrosse, Catholic-on-paper (Mater Dei).
  Charming, entitled, not evil; wants what he wants. **(Revised after S1E11:)** never Lena's rebound (she goes from Wes to Julian).
  He is Theo's Sigma Rho big when Theo rushes (S2) and Laney's date to Pi Lam's winter formal (Sat Jan 30, 2010 — before New Orleans),
  which ends with Laney leaving early in Theo's car.
- **Dale Wisniewski**, 58 (added E05 plan). Warren, *Michigan* ("not the other one"); 27 years at Chrysler's Warren Truck
  Assembly (Dodge Rams), laid off in the spring of the Chrysler bankruptcy; moved into his daughter's spare room in Marisol
  in July. Barry Sanders jersey, a Lions fan through 0–16, sits alone at the end of Sully's bar on Sundays. Lena adopts him
  at the opener (the Lions are the Aints of the North). May recur on Saints Sundays as Lena's charity case and Jack's mirror
  for Frank. Never a sad-sack: dry, gentle, tips in quarters.
- **Gloria Mendes**, 63, night waitress at the Anchor diner for 30 years. Rates customers like pies (Jack: "Lattice-top.
  Pretty on top. Everything's leaking underneath."). Hires Jack as night dishwasher (S1E02: "Four out of ten? Hon, you're
  hired."): Tue/Thu/Sat, 7 p.m.–1 a.m., $8.00/hr (California minimum, 2009) plus a share of tips.
- **Tom and Rosa (Gutiérrez) Calloway**, Nora's parents, raisin farmers near Selma (Fresno County). 🔒 Their parish in
  Selma whispered suicide. Rosa has not been to Communion since — she believes she failed her daughter. Appear S4–S5.
- **Carly Benedetto**, 19, Youngstown, Jack's friend from Mooney, at Youngstown State. 🔒 The girl Jack hit Mikey
  Kostelnik for (§16, revised: Mikey was her ex, drunk, hitting her and dragging her toward his truck; no sexual
  element). She knows the truth. Appears in Youngstown episodes.
- **Michael "Mikey" Kostelnik**, 20, Struthers, Ohio. 🔒 The young man whose skull hit the cinderblocks. Traumatic brain
  injury: seizures, slowed speech, can't drive or work, lives with his mother Donna. Jack sends anonymous postal money
  orders to Donna Kostelnik every month from his Anchor wages.
- **Verna** (added E11 plan), seventies, keeps the **Santa Lucia Motor Court** (fictional: six cabins on a shelf of the mountain a mile south of Lucia, Big
  Sur; a VACANCY sign with the NO burned out; a payphone on the post by the office under a bare bulb; a muted TV in the office (the ALCS, rained out in New York — Researcher)). Cardigan over a
  flannel nightgown; a cigarette she never lights; $40 a cabin; coffee in an urn at seven. "Heater works in four. One heater. It's a long story and I'm not
  telling it." She never tells it.
- **Father Bede** (added E11 plan), seventies, a Camaldolese monk of the New Camaldoli Hermitage (a fictional person at a real house): white habit, a voice
  like gravel in a coffee can; preaches two sentences on Bartimaeus (Sun Oct 25). Never recurs unless the show goes back up the hill.

# PART III — THE HIDDEN ARCHITECTURE

## 13. Hidden prior knowledge at the pilot (Knowledge Auditor: read this)

At 6:48 a.m. on August 29, 2009, before the first line of the pilot, these people already know things the player doesn't:

- **Lena** knows: Nora Calloway (her Preview Weekend host, April 24–26, 2009); the Lighthouse house and the path to the
  Point (Nora walked her past it on Friday night, April 24); Nora was pregnant (the test in the trash); that she herself
  removed and discarded the test; that she told police she slept through; that around 12:45 a.m. she heard a boy crying
  in the Serra Hall corridor and a door shut, and didn't look. Fr. Osei (she confessed to him April 26). Mrs. Fitch (met
  at the Preview brunch). Hale (postcard, 2008). She does **not** know Theo, Kevin, Evan, Julian (she knows *of* Julian
  — Nora mentioned "my friend Jules who writes songs"), or Jack.
- **Theo** knows: what he did; Kevin (the beach stairs, the floor of Kevin's host's room); Fr. Osei (his confession, July
  2009); the Lighthouse; the Point; Nora's name (from the news). He has never seen Lena (she was asleep in Serra; he never
  saw her). He has Nora's jacket in a duffel in his closet.
- **Kevin** knows: Theo, and what Theo looked like at 12:45 a.m. He does not know what happened. He knows Nora's name
  from the news and the Newman memorial Mass. He has seen Lena once (the Preview Weekend Newman brunch, across the room)
  and might recognize her. He knows Fr. Osei (Preview brunch; a summer email).
- **Fr. Osei** knows: Lena (her confession); Theo (his confession); Kevin (brunch); Nora (his parishioner and soprano).

he says nothing; she doesn't recognize him). Lena and Osei (*not* pretended — Osei greets her warmly: "Magdalena. You
came back." She explains to Jack: "Preview weekend. In April." True).

## 15. The elision rule (how close third keeps Jack's secret honestly)

Jack is the POV and the player *is* Jack, so the narration must never lie about what Jack did. It may **elide**: it can
decline to narrate a memory. Jack thinks about Danny constantly; the narration says so. It shows Jack flinching from the
scar story, smoking when Danny's name comes up, sending money orders to a "D. Kostelnik" (S1E05, a detail with no
explanation), writing letters he doesn't send. It never says "Danny did it" in Jack's voice; it never says "Jack did it"
until the reveal. When the truth comes out (S2E22), every earlier scene must read as honest in retrospect: the player
should feel *Jack was carrying this the whole time and I was carrying it with him without knowing.*
- Allowed: "Jack doesn't think about that night. He's good at it."
- Not allowed: "Jack has never hurt anyone."

## 16. The eyebrow scar — canon of the tellings

Jack tells the story differently every time. Track every telling in `continuity.md`. Never repeat a version with the same
person. The **truth** (🔒; revised for the brief's hard rule — no sexual element): November 23, 2007, a bonfire in the lot
behind the dead Republic Steel works in Struthers; Mikey Kostelnik, 18, drunk, Carly Benedetto's ex-boyfriend of two
weeks, had already backhanded her once in front of everyone and had her by the wrist and the hair, dragging her toward
his pickup to drive her home himself, drunk, while she screamed that she wasn't going (🔒 the drunk driver Jack stopped —
a rhyme with Laney's Kaylee and Hale's two drivers, never stated); Jack, 16, pulled him off; Mikey swung first, wearing his
Struthers High class ring, and split Jack's left eyebrow; Jack hit him once, with everything; Mikey went down backward and
his head struck the cinderblock fire ring. Danny, 20, arrived at the run, took one look, took Jack's bloody hand and wiped
it on his own shirt, and when the Struthers police came said, "It was me."
**Tellings used on the played path (after S1E02):** Theo — swan; Lena — pierogi, Archbishop; Osei — "a fight, a long
time ago" (honest, partial); the floor — none. **E03** offers Margot her first (she asks "for the file"); the Pacific one is
saved for whoever asks in the water.
**After S1E03:** Margot — Sister Mary Clement, third grade, a ruler (`scar_told_margot=="nun"`; "Predictable," underlined
twice; it is on her whiteboard file from E04). Nobody asked in the water. **E04** offers no telling (a grief week; nobody
asks). Still unused with Lena: the hockey puck, the husband, the swan, the Franciscan, the Pacific, Gina's controller.
**After S1E04:** no telling (as planned). **E05** offers **Kevin** his first (fork B, the Brick, when his ER story sends
Jack's hand to the eyebrow): bank options for him — the hockey puck, the Franciscan, the Pacific, or the honest partial "a
fight, a long time ago" (Osei's version; Kevin is the second person to get it). Never the girl.
**After S1E05:** no telling (pref fork; Kevin was never asked). **E06** offers **Becca** her first, at the waterline during
the cleanup — **"I walked into the Pacific Ocean"** is finally the one for whoever asks in the water; also the Franciscan
(charm), Gina's controller, or the honest partial "a fight, a long time ago" (Becca would be the second to get it, after
Osei). Kevin's first remains banked.
**After S1E06:** Becca → **the Franciscan** (`scar_told_becca=="franciscan"`; Eli and Father Anselm — "It's always the monks").
The Pacific is still banked for whoever asks in the water. **E07** offers no telling (nobody asks; the misread week is not
Kevin's week). Still unused with Lena: the hockey puck, the husband, the swan, the Franciscan, the Pacific, Gina's controller.
**After S1E07:** no telling (as planned). **E08** offers none on the page: on the moonlit cove Lena may *look* at the eyebrow and
not ask (one clause, if anywhere) — the next telling she gets should be later and should cost more. Kevin's first remains banked
(S1E16 is his week); "A girl. That one's true." stays reserved for Lena, much later.
**After S1E08:** no telling (as planned). **E09** offers **Wes** his first, *in the water* at Campus Point at 2 a.m. — "I walked
into the Pacific Ocean" at last finds its listener (the man who came to see the Pacific); also Gina's controller or the hockey puck.
**Never "a fight, a long time ago" in a scene where Danny is mentioned** (elision: the scar and Danny's fight never sit side by side).
**After S1E09:** Wes → **Gina's N64 controller** (`scar_told_wes=="controller"`; "I'd believe it of any sister"). The Pacific is
**still** banked for whoever asks in the water. **E10** offers no telling (a fight, a bell tower; nobody asks). Kevin's first stays banked
(S1E16).
**After S1E10:** no telling (as planned). **E11** offers **Laney** her first, **in the water** at Pfeiffer Beach (fork B): **"I walked into the Pacific
Ocean"** at last (banked since E03 "for whoever asks in the water"); or the swan (Laney heard Theo's at the Malibu pool — she catches it); or "Ask Lena.
She's got two."; or the honest partial "a fight, a long time ago" (no Danny anywhere near it — the elision rule). Kevin's first stays banked (S1E16).
**After S1E11:** Laney → **the swan** (`scar_told_laney=="swan"` — caught as Theo's: "He told me on the Aerobed"). **The Pacific is still banked**
"for whoever asks in the water." **E12** offers no telling (a party of costumes; nobody asks a farmer about his eyebrow). Kevin's first stays banked
(S1E16). Still unused with Lena: the hockey puck, the husband, the swan, the Franciscan, the Pacific, Gina's controller.
🔒 **The hand (for the elision):** Jack is right-handed; the punch was his right. When Mark 9:43 is read (Sun Sep 27, 2009 —
"If your hand causes you to sin, cut it off"), the narration may let his right hand do one small thing and say nothing about
why.
**Tellings bank** (each usable once per listener): a hockey puck at a Youngstown Phantoms game; a nun with a ruler (Sr.
Mary Clement, 3rd grade, "she was aiming for my soul"); a jealous husband in Erie, Pennsylvania; a swan in Mill Creek Park;
knife-juggling at the St. Anthony's pierogi festival (the Ukrainians did it); a Franciscan who "plays dirty in pickup";
"I walked into the Pacific Ocean"; "My sister, with a Nintendo 64 controller" (Gina will confirm it, falsely, if asked —
she covers for him without knowing why); a cat named Archbishop; "A girl. That one's true." (He says that last one only
to Lena, much later, and it is — Carly — and it isn't.)

## 17. JACK'S SECRET (full)

**What happened:** §16. Mikey Kostelnik lived. He has a traumatic brain injury. Struthers PD found Danny with blood on his
shirt and Jack's scholarship-track future on the bench in the back of the cruiser. Danny had a prior (a 2006 bar fight,
misdemeanor assault) and was on probation; the Mahoning County prosecutor charged felonious assault; Danny took a plea
for five years in January 2008. At the station at 4 a.m., Jack, sixteen, tried once to tell a detective the truth; Frank
walked in and took him out of the room by the back of his neck. On the bench in the hall, Jack prayed for the first time
in his life like someone was there. He has been praying like that ever since. **His faith was born the night he became a
coward.** That's the knot.

**Why it matters to the show:** Jack spends seasons judging Theo's silence — and Lena's — while living inside his own. The
central mystery is a mirror: everyone who loves Theo will be asked what Jack's family already chose. Hale's specialty is
*moral luck* (Williams, Nagel): the idea that we are judged for outcomes we didn't control. Jack threw one punch in
defense of a girl; Mikey hit a cinderblock. Theo grabbed a girl to save her; she fell. Hale's seminar will put the
question to Jack in the abstract in S1E05 and he will argue it like his life depends on it, and the player won't know
why yet.

**The money orders.** Jack sends $20–$60 monthly to Donna Kostelnik, unsigned. Shown without explanation from S1E05.

**The clock.** 🔒 Jack is aware, vaguely and with dread, that there are time limits on prosecutions, and that if he does
nothing long enough, nothing can be done. *Writers: never specify the law on the page. The dread is moral, not legal.*
The show's position: he will tell, and the telling (S7, Fall 2012, to Mikey's mother first, then to the Mahoning County
prosecutor) costs him; what the law does about it is less important than what Donna Kostelnik does — 🔒 she slams the
door, and then, in S8, she comes to Danny's welcome-home Mass.

**Danny's release:** judicial release granted November 2011 (S5) — or the full term to January 2013, depending on whether
Jack has told the truth by then (S5 path flag). Danny at Jack's graduation, May 2013. Danny as godfather to Jack's first
child. **(Re-planned after S1E11:)** Lena knows the truth before the proposal (S5 at the latest; the player may tell her from S3).
Danny stands up at the wedding (July 21, 2012) only if he is out; if not, Jack and Lena drive to Trumbull's visiting room the
week they're home, in the suit and the dress (room's call) — the path's price, paid in a cinderblock room.

## 18. LENA'S SECRETS (full)

**A. The Preview Weekend (tied to the central mystery).**
- April 24, 2009 (Friday): Lena, 17, arrives for Marisol Preview Weekend. St. Brendan's runs a hosting program for Catholic
  admits; her host is **Nora Calloway** (Serra Hall 3B; Sloane was away for the night at the Pi Lam formal in Santa
  Barbara). Nora is luminous, funny, kind, a soprano; they stay up talking about Catherine of Siena and boys. Nora walks
  her past the Lighthouse to the Point at sunset on Friday and shows her the wall: "This is where I come to yell at God."
  Lena falls a little in love with the idea of becoming her.
- April 25 (Saturday): Nora is distracted all day; disappears for an hour after lunch (she bought the test at Walgreens,
  2:14 p.m.). Newman admit dinner 6–8 p.m. At 10:30 p.m. Nora goes to "a party for an hour"; asks Lena to come; Lena
  declines (she's seventeen, tired, and a good girl in a strange town). Nora returns at 11:15, shaking, goes into the
  bathroom; Lena, pretending to sleep, hears her crying and praying the Memorare. At 11:40 Nora kneels by Lena's air
  mattress and whispers: *"Cover for me, Magdalena. If anyone asks, I'm in the shower."* Leaves.
- 11:45 p.m.: Lena gets up for water and sees the test in the bathroom trash. Two lines.
- ~12:45 a.m.: Lena hears a boy crying in the corridor — a broken, gasping sound — then a door down the hall open and shut.
  She doesn't look. She's covering.
- April 26, 7:40 a.m.: the Serra RA knocks: there's been an accident. Before police arrive, Lena takes the test out of the
  trash, wraps it in a Newman bulletin, walks it to the dumpster behind Serra Hall, and comes back. To Det. Ochoa she says
  she was asleep by eleven and Nora was "fine. A little sad. Normal sad." She touches her grandmother's medal the whole
  time.
- April 26, 2:00 p.m.: she goes to St. Brendan's and makes her confession to Fr. Osei: she lied to the police and she
  threw away something that belonged to a dead girl. He absolves her and tells her, gently and firmly, that she should
  tell the police the truth. She doesn't. She tells herself she's protecting a dead girl's name and a family in Fresno. 🔒
  The irony she doesn't know: the autopsy already told the police about the pregnancy. Her sin hid nothing. Her guilt is
  real anyway.
- She turns down Notre Dame (partial aid) for Marisol (Regents' Scholarship, full tuition) and tells everyone it was the
  money. It was also Nora: "I owe her four years."
- She never told anyone about the boy crying in the corridor. She has thought about him every night.

**B. Grandmère and Memorial (family rupture).** See §10.9. Lena knows her grandmother died at Memorial during Katrina; she
knows her mother was working in the building and left on a boat on the fourth day; she has never asked her mother a single
question about it. She has read every public article about the Memorial deaths and hates herself for it. Celeste's text
on August 30, 2009 (*"Don't read the Times this week, cher."*) is the first time her mother has acknowledged that there is
anything to read.

**C. Wes.** She knows something's wrong. She has noticed he hasn't mentioned Mass since Labor Day. She's decided not to
notice. **(Re-planned after S1E11:)** On All Souls (S1E13) she asks, and he tells her, and ends it (§10.1).

**D. The postcard.** Hale wrote her the same line he wrote Jack. She came, in part, to say it to his face.

## 20. THE CENTRAL MYSTERY — "THE POINT" 🔒

The player chose "surprise me completely." This is the spine that will entangle the ensemble from the pilot through at
least S5, with consequences through the full run.

### 20.1 The surface (what Marisol believes)
At about 6:10 a.m. on **Sunday, April 26, 2009** (the Third Sunday of Easter), two surfers paddling out at the Point break
found the body of **Nora Grace Calloway**, 19, a sophomore, on the rocks below the old Point Marisol Light. The coroner
found a BAC of 0.09 and a therapeutic level of alprazolam. Her phone was in her jeans, destroyed. Marisol PD (Det. Sgt.
Ray Ochoa) ruled it an accidental fall: she was known to sit on the seaward side of the low wall at night. The campus held
a vigil. St. Brendan's held a memorial Mass. A white wooden cross stands at the wall, and every Sunday before dawn
someone leaves fresh flowers there; nobody knows who. Privately, many — including her parents' parish in Selma, the
detective, Julian, and later Margot — believe it was suicide.

### 20.2 Who she was
Nora Grace Calloway, born **September 8, 1989** (the Nativity of Mary) in Selma, California. Father Tom, a raisin farmer;
mother Rosa (Gutiérrez), a school-district bookkeeper; two younger brothers (Mateo, 15; Sam, 12). Philosophy major, Music
minor, Newman choir soprano. Funny, generous, intense, devout, doubting, brave. Freshman year in Delgado 222 with Priya;
sophomore year in Serra 3B with Sloane. She wore a Levi's jean jacket with a **Fresno Fair pin** on the collar and an
embroidered **Our Lady of Guadalupe patch** sewn on the back by her grandmother. She sang with Julian at the Point wall.
She sent Hale a rebuttal in high school and got his postcard. She took his seminar. She fell in love with his TA.

### 20.3 The truth of the night (timeline)
- **Oct 2008–Apr 2009:** Nora and Evan Marsh, secretly together.
- **Sat Apr 25, 2:14 p.m.:** Walgreens on Mission Street: pregnancy test. Positive (about 8 weeks).
- **6:00–8:00 p.m.:** Newman admit dinner (Nora hosts Lena).
- **~8:30 p.m.:** Nora texts Evan the news. He calls; they fight — he panics, says "we can't," says "there are options."
- **10:30 p.m.:** Nora goes to the Lighthouse party (the last house on Seacliff Road, rented by senior surfers). Julian is
  there with his guitar; they sing one song on the stairs ("Skinny Love"). Rafi Ochoa, a high-school senior then, is
  selling. Nora buys two Xanax bars from him: she hasn't slept in a week. She takes one with a beer. Sloane sees her leave
  at ~10:50, crying.
- **Also at the Lighthouse:** Theo Vance, 18, an admit, crashing with an LA friend's older brother who lives there. Around
  10:45 he goes down the bluff stairs to the beach to smoke and meets **Kevin Tran**, 18, an admit, who wandered out of
  the party because he couldn't breathe. They talk for an hour. They kiss. Kevin leaves ~11:50 for his host's room in
  Serra Hall, shaking with joy and terror. Theo stays, does two lines of cocaine alone on the stairs, and walks out along
  the bluff toward the light.
- **11:15 p.m.:** Nora back at Serra; the bathroom; the Memorare. **11:40:** "Cover for me, Magdalena." **11:45:** Lena sees
  the test.
- **11:52 p.m.:** On the Point path, Nora calls Hale. Forty-one seconds. *"Go home, Nora. You're drunk and you're boring
  me."*
- **12:05 a.m.:** Nora texts Evan: *"I'm at the Point. Please come. I'm keeping it. I want to keep it. I want you to want
  it."* She sits on the seaward side of the wall, legs over the drop, as she has done a hundred times, and prays aloud.
- **~12:25 a.m.:** Theo, high, comes up the path in the dark and sees a girl on the outside of the wall, talking to
  someone who isn't there. He thinks she's about to jump. He runs at her and grabs her from behind by the jacket. She
  startles, twists, her hand slips off the wall; the jacket pulls off her shoulders as she goes; he's left holding it. She
  doesn't scream. He hears her hit. The last words he heard her say were *"…never was it known that anyone who fled to thy
  protection…"*
- **~12:27–12:31:** Theo, in shock, runs. Down the Point path toward Seacliff Road, the jacket clutched to his chest.
- **12:20:** Evan reads the text and drives. **12:31:** at the Point lot his headlights catch a tall blond boy running
  toward Seacliff Road holding something dark. Evan goes to the wall; it's black; he calls her phone (it's under her, in
  the surf, already dead); he waits ten minutes, decides she's left to punish him, drives home. He deletes the text the
  next morning. He keeps a screenshot on his laptop he took at 12:21 to "show her later how scared he'd been." It's still
  there.
- **~12:45 a.m.:** Theo walks to Serra Hall — the only place on campus he knows anyone — and knocks on Kevin's host's door
  (Serra 3F, two doors from Nora's room), soaked to the knees from crossing the creek mouth, palms scraped raw from the
  path, holding the jacket. He's crying. *"You never saw me."* Kevin lets him in; he sleeps on the floor; he's gone at
  dawn. **This is the crying boy Lena heard.**
- **6:10 a.m.:** surfers find her.

### 20.4 The layers of guilt (no villains)
Every person who touched that night believes, privately, that they killed Nora Calloway:
- **Theo** — he did, by trying to save her. The truest guilt and the least culpable intent.
- **Evan** — he said "there are options," didn't answer at 12:05, saw the boy, drove away, lied.
- **Hale** — forty-one seconds.
- **Lena** — she covered for her; she didn't stop her; she hid the test; she didn't look at the crying boy.
- **Rafi** — the Xanax.
- **Kevin** — he let Theo in and never asked.
- **Julian** — he didn't see she was drowning.
- **Sloane** — she let her walk out of the party crying.
- **Priya** — they drifted.
- **Ray Ochoa** — he closed it as an accident while believing it was suicide, out of mercy, and that mercy kept the truth
  buried.

The show's answer, delivered over seasons and never as a speech: everyone did a small wrong thing, the fall was an
accident, and Nora **was not trying to die** — she had chosen to keep her child and was praying the Memorare on the wall
where she always prayed. The proof is two things only a guilty man and a coward kept: Evan's screenshot of the text, and
Theo's memory of the prayer. The truth heals one person completely — Rosa Calloway, her mother, who has believed for two
years that her daughter killed herself and her grandchild. In S5, Rosa receives Communion for the first time since April
2009. That's the payoff the whole mystery is built for.

### 20.5 Physical evidence and clues
- **The jean jacket** (Theo's closet, in a black Prada duffel on the top shelf). Levi's, women's small, Fresno Fair pin
  on the collar, Guadalupe patch on the back. Inside pocket: **Hale's 2007 postcard to Nora** (Radcliffe Camera; "Come and
  say it to my face. —A.H."), folded soft; a Walgreens receipt (Apr 25, 2:14 p.m.: First Response, $16.99); a St. Brendan's
  choir schedule. Police report notes "victim reportedly wearing a denim jacket; not recovered; presumed lost to the sea."
- **Phone records:** 11:52 p.m. call to Hale (41 sec); 12:05 a.m. text to Evan Marsh. Ochoa interviewed both. Hale said she
  was upset about a paper. Evan said he was asleep and saw the text in the morning. Content of the text never retrieved.
- **Evan's laptop:** the 12:21 screenshot.
- **The cross and the Sunday flowers:** Theo. Every Sunday before dawn. Supermarket roses in winter; jasmine from Las Olas
  fences in summer.
- **Nora's journal** (Priya's box): last entry April 23: *"If I tell him and he says no, I'll still say yes. Lord, to whom
  shall we go."* (John 6:68.) Priya opens it in S2.
- **Julian's song "Fresno":** a line, *"you said the wall was where you yelled at God / I never heard him yell back"* —
  and a verse about her jacket.
- **The postcards:** Jack's, Lena's, Nora's. Same line, same handwriting, same Radcliffe Camera image. Hale's habit.
- **Lena's memory:** the boy crying in the corridor at Serra Hall; a door two down the hall.
- **Kevin's Preview Weekend host:** Serra 3F, two doors from 3B. In the housing records Margot pulls (S3).
- **Evan's sighting:** tall, blond, running, carrying something dark.
- **The Lighthouse:** the senior surfers rotate out; in Fall 2009 it's rented by a new crew who throw the season-opening
  party (the pilot). A photo from April 25 on a Facebook album ("LIGHTHOUSE LAST HURRAH 4/25") shows Theo in the
  background, by the stairs, at 10:41 p.m. Margot finds it in S3.

### 20.6 Red herrings (fair, each true about something)
1. **Hale did it** (S2): the postcard in the jacket points at him; his drinking, his possessiveness, his lie about the
   call. He didn't push her; he did fail her.
2. **Suicide** (S1–S2): the pregnancy, the Xanax, the crying, "cover for me," the town's whisper. Wrong — and the most
   cruel wrong.
3. **Evan pushed her** (S2–S3): he was there; he lied; he's unstable. He didn't.
4. **Julian** (S2, briefly; Jack's jealousy makes him want it): he loved her, he wrote the song, he knows the wall. No.
5. **Sloane / Pi Lam hazing** (S1E12, Laney's arc): a rumor the sorority made pledges walk the wall; false, though the
   rumor is real and leads to the Halloween scare.
6. **Rafi's pills killed her** (S2): the Xanax made her unsteady. Partly true; not the cause.

### 20.7 Seed plan — what lands when
**Pilot (S1E01) — invisible seeds only:**
- Priya's floor-meeting rule: nobody goes to the Point after dark.
- At the end of the Lighthouse driveway, a man older than everyone, smoking, staring at the house, not coming in (Evan).
  Gone later.
- Theo and Kevin's "first" handshake held a second too long; Theo quiet for one line; Kevin's smile.
- Rafi: "Not bars, though. I don't do bars anymore."
- Julian on the stairs with a guitar; Lena stopping to listen with an expression Jack can't read.
- On the back porch Lena knows the gap in the fence and the path to the Point and warns Jack off it; when he asks how she
  knows, she says Laney's sorority friend told her — and touches her medal.
- Jack's paperback drops Hale's postcard; Lena reads it; something happens in her face; she asks "Who's A.H.?"; Jack
  deflects. (She has the same card.)
- If Jack goes to the cove with Theo: Theo, looking north at the dark cliff, asks about hell and the exchange rate on
  forgiveness. Theo leaves with Sloane.
- Mrs. Fitch: "We lost our best soprano last spring."
- After Mass, Lena lights a candle at the Marian shrine beside a small brass plate: NORA GRACE CALLOWAY · 1989–2009 · "LORD,
  TO WHOM SHALL WE GO?"
- Osei to Lena: "Magdalena. You came back."
- Tag: Theo comes in before dawn with sand on his feet and wet hems. "Couldn't sleep. Went to look at the ocean."
- 🔒 It is Sunday morning. He was leaving the flowers.

**S1 seeds and turns:**
- E02 (re-planned after S1E01): Hale's first seminar — Phineas Gage ("Gage was no longer Gage"; Jack's hand goes to the
  eyebrow; 🔒 Mikey); if Jack's heart answer, John 6:68 aloud (the plaque's words; Hale: "I was made to learn it"). Caleb:
  "He wrote you *back*? I wrote him four letters" — Lena silent beside them. Hale recognizes the girl who fled the Anchor
  (Lena's medal lie: "something on the stove"). Evan is the driveway man and the Thursday TA; if Jack mentions the
  Lighthouse, Evan says he doesn't go to parties and asks only who Jack came with. Facebook "People You May Know": a candle,
  "Nora Calloway · 1 mutual friend (Priya Raman)" (no Lena, Julian or Sloane anywhere on it). Wes: a status about the Army
  Corps of Engineers; a "like" at 3:58 a.m. Central; the first missed 8:04 call. Kevin's smooth lie ("Met him at the floor
  meeting"). Rudy's mother, three buses every other Sunday (🔒 rhymes with Maria and Danny). Doug last out at the alarm.
- E03 (re-planned after S1E02; Labor Day weekend in Malibu): **Theo keeps the Sunday flowers even from Malibu** — the SL
  is gone from the drive before dawn Sunday (Margot, who doesn't sleep, saw it leave at 2:40) and back by the end of the
  8 a.m. Mass, dew on the paint, Theo outside the church with coffees: "I'm a supporter, not a member" (🔒 three hours up,
  the flowers, three hours back; and he has been inside St. Brendan's exactly once, in July). The pilot's unasked cove
  question is re-seeded in Theo's idiom when Jack pays him the pie: "Does He take IOUs? Your God. Or is it cash on
  delivery." Theo hears Danny's call and keeps it — the first secret Theo keeps *for* Jack (🔒 the mirror: the keeper of the
  worst secret on campus is good at it). Kevin and Theo at opposite ends of the pool at 1 a.m., feet in the water, not

*(S1E12–S5 seeds re-planned after S1E11 for the player's MAJOR RE-PLAN; the mystery's spine and every hidden arc are unchanged —
only their timing against the romance moves.)*
- E12 (Halloween; **re-planned after S1E11** — full plan `outlines/s1e12.md`), none glossed: (1) **Evan**, walking his bike on Del Playa, sees
  Theo under the Ferrante house's floodlights and goes white — "Who is that?" (Jack's answer routes E19). (2) **Sloane's pregame lore** sends three
  pledges and Laney toward the wall; they get as far as Lupe's (red herring #5, spent comically; Sloane's "before" never explained). (3) **Lena stops at
  the last house** (medal) and Jack goes up alone — **his first time at the Point** (`knows_point`): the old light, the low wall, **the cross with NORA**,
  Oct 18's flowers dead on kitchen string (Big Sur broke the Sunday; no fresh ones). (4) **Theo at the wall at the second one o'clock** (DST), drunk,
  hands flat on the stones, a Krug at his foot: "Don't. Okay? Just — not there." (`saw_theo_wall`). Reckless path: Jack sits up on the wall and **Theo
  hauls him off it** — the grab, and this time it works. Theo leaves the Krug at the cross. (5) **"Tell them I was looking for Laney."** — Margot saw
  him leave at 12:30. (6) **Lena in the duel: "No. I never have."** — medal (the lie: Nora walked her there at sunset, April 24). (7) **Theo's toast:**
  "Can't repeat the past? Tonight, at two o'clock, you can. Once."; the widow's walk (fork): the dead Point light — "It's not lit, Margot. Look." (8)
  **Kevin's white mask** all night. (9) **Sol** films the boy in the straw hat; "Soledad. Don't." (10) **Dawn:** Theo asleep in the dinner jacket, sand
  to the knees (the Sunday flowers after all; no line). Nora's name is **read** (the crossbar), never spoken.
- E13 (All Souls): the Book of the Dead — Lena writes *Lucille Guidry* and *Nora Calloway*; Jack sees: "She was my host at
  Preview Weekend." True; partial. **An hour later Julian writes *Nora Calloway* in the same book** (🔒 two people who loved her; one
  hiding what she knows); Lena reads his hand. That night Wes tells her the attic — the knocking no one answered (the theme, not
  the mystery) — and ends it.
- E14: the postcards: Jack and Lena discover they both have Hale's card.
- E15–E16: Jack's jealousy makes Julian a suspect in his own head (red herring #4, moved up from S2): Julian at the wall at dusk
  with his guitar case; the way Lena listens to him.
- E16: the Kevin/Theo stairwell fight Jack half-hears: "You said you'd never—" "I haven't."
- E19: Evan asks Jack, "Your roommate — was he at Preview Weekend last April?"
- E20: the jacket again — Jack notices the Fresno Fair pin.
- E21: Sol, in 412 with the Bolex, pans across the closet's top shelf ("What's in the black one, Teddy?" — "Shoes."); one image (🔒).
- **E22 (season cliffhanger):** On the last night before break, Lena shows Jack a photo on her laptop: Nora at the Point
  wall, laughing, in her jean jacket — the Guadalupe patch, the Fresno Fair pin. "They never found it. Her mom asked me in
  a letter if I remembered what she was wearing." Jack has seen that jacket. Smash to black.

**S2 (Spring 2010):** Over Christmas Theo moves the duffel (his mother's pool house in La Cañada). **S2E03:** Jack checks the
closet — gone. He investigates in silence, alone, while his life fills with Sol, the Saints and New Orleans; Margot starts the
anniversary feature (S2E12) and, later, he with her. On the trip, one beat only: Luc, NOPD, at the Broussards' kitchen table,
on how cops close a case they feel sorry for (🔒 Ray Ochoa's mercy, unnamed). **S2E16:** the duffel is back on the shelf after
Easter; Jack opens it and finds Hale's postcard to Nora in the jacket's inside pocket → the Hale suspicion (red herring #1),
sharpened because Lena has just beaten Hale at the Forum (S2E13). **S2E17:** Priya opens the journal (the April 23 entry). **S2E18,
the anniversary (Sun Apr 25 – Mon Apr 26, 2010, Good Shepherd Sunday):** Evan confronts Theo at the cross before dawn with the
flowers in his hand; Jack, following Theo for the first time, intervenes; Evan knows now. **S2E22:** Theo tells Jack the truth on
the wall. Jack, in exchange — because it's the only thing he can offer — tells Theo about Danny. (The player learns both truths in
the same scene.) **Jack is Lena's boyfriend by then, and now he carries a secret that isn't his.**

**S3 (Fall 2010):** Jack holds Theo's secret while Theo holds himself together in public — and holds it from Lena, his girlfriend;
Margot dates Evan and finds the screenshot; Lena confides about the test (E18, Thanksgiving in New Orleans, the kitchen at 1 a.m.)
— to Jack, who can't tell her what he knows. Kevin comes out to Jack and Lena.

**S4 (Spring 2011):** Jack and Lena together, with the secret between them. Margot's reporting closes in. Evan goes to the
Calloways. Kevin decides to testify. **April 24, 2011, Easter Sunday:** Theo goes to Mass with Jack. **April 26, 2011:** Theo walks
into Marisol PD with the jacket and Ray Ochoa takes his statement. The same week Lena's own part comes out (the host, the test,
the crying boy in the Serra corridor — it was Theo), and Lena learns Jack knew for a year. **The rupture (S4E21–E22)** — a
separation, never a breakup with anyone else in it (brief: no more other relationships), that holds all summer.

**S5 (Fall 2011):** consequences. The DA's decision (the grab was a rescue attempt; the flight is the problem; drug
possession; a misdemeanor plea and community service — the law shrugs, and the shrug is its own horror for Theo). The
press realizes Fr. Osei may have known; the seal on trial; Osei jailed for contempt. Rosa Calloway receives Communion. Jack and
Lena, after Wes's funeral, rebuild on the far side of all of it.

**Full run:** the case's after-life — Theo's film, Evan's prison teaching, Ray Ochoa's retirement, the cross at the
Point removed by the county in 2014 and replaced by a bench with a plaque that Theo pays for anonymously: *"Never was it
known."*

## 21. ARC REGISTRY

Duration classes: **EP** (one episode) · **ME** (multi-episode, 2–6) · **SEA** (a season) · **MS** (multi-season) ·
**RUN** (the whole show). Episode codes: S1E05 = Season 1, Episode 5; SU1 = Summer 2010; SU2 = Summer 2011; SU3 = Summer
2012; AD = adult seasons. Update the status column after every played episode.

| # | Arc | Class | Start | Turn | Payoff | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Jack & Lena: the romance | RUN | S1E01 | S1E13 Wes ends it (Jack two days from Becca official); S1E19 Jack free, Lena with Julian; S1E22 the roof ("When nobody has to be the other anything"); S2 Sol; **S2E10 New Orleans — the honesty**; S2E11 "No." / "Do it properly."; **S2E15 official + first kiss (Easter Vigil, Apr 3, 2010)**; S4E21–22 the rupture | **S5E22 proposal (Gaudete week 2011)**; S6E09 Ephesians 5; **SU3 wedding (Sat July 21, 2012)**; S7–S8 married, living together | open · **re-planned after S1E11** (MAJOR RE-PLAN + exact sequence + long-range timeline) |

## 22. SEASON 1 — FALL 2009 (22 episodes, Aug 29 – Dec 18, 2009)

*Liturgical year B through Christ the King (Nov 22); Year C from the First Sunday of Advent (Nov 29). Classes begin Mon
Aug 31; Labor Day Sept 7; Thanksgiving break Nov 25–29; finals Dec 14–18.*

**E01 · "Lip Service"** · Sat Aug 29 – Sun Aug 30 · 22nd Sunday OT (Mark 7). Ted Kennedy's funeral Mass in Boston; the
Station Fire above La Cañada; Frank sees the Pacific. Move-in, Theo, the floor meeting; the Lighthouse party; the back
porch; Mass at St. Brendan's; the first duel; Hale in a diner booth; the stairwell call ("Wes"); sand on Theo's feet.

**E02 · "The Examined Life"** · Mon Aug 31 – Fri Sep 4 · St. Gregory the Great (Thu Sept 3; weekday Gospel Luke 5:1–11,
"Depart from me, Lord, for I am a sinful man"); Ramadan; full moon Fri Sept 4. *(Re-planned after S1E01; full plan in
`outlines/s1e02.md`.)* Cold open: the first 3 a.m. fire alarm, the whole floor on the lawn; Theo meets Lena ("I like her.
She's mean"). Hale's PHIL 9H: "Prove to me you have a soul" — a four-move duel **against Hale**, Lena as rival and ally
(Ryle's ghost; 🔒 Phineas Gage; Lena vs. Jack on form vs. interior; the genetic debunking); Hale pairs Jack and Lena as
**designated antagonists** for the semester (weekly papers, annotated in each other's margins). New at the table: Caleb
Hargrove, Yusuf Noorzai. Margot meets Lena in PHIL 1 and says "useful idiots" aloud. Gloria hires Jack ("Four out of ten?
Hon, you're hired"); Rudy Salcedo; Rafi lends him La Flaca. Frank home ("How's the pelican?"); Traficant; the letter to
Danny (elided). Osei: "the box or the board" (fork). Daily Mass; Kevin's lunch (a smooth lie about Theo). Evan's Thursday
section — the driveway man. Lena names Wes to Jack's face; the second cigarette ("I'm going to need a container").
Facebook: In a Relationship; 🔒 Nora's candle; 🔒 Wes awake at 3:58 a.m. Laney's Pi Lam open house (fork: with Lena / with
Margot and Kevin). The full-moon beach; Lena teaches the Who Dat. Theo's 450SL and the pitch: "Full moon, Costigan. LA is
three hours. Get in the car." (`e2_la`). Becca invites Jack to the Sept 19 BBQ.

**E03 · "Labor"** · Sat Sep 5 – Mon Sep 7 (Labor Day) · 23rd Sunday OT (Is 35; **James 2:1-5**, the rich man with
the gold ring; Mark 7:31-37, *Ephphatha*). *(Re-planned after S1E02 for the played path — `e2_la=="broke"`; full plan in
`outlines/s1e03.md`.)* **Player note: Lena lighter and funnier — a breather after E02's ache, so the next ache (E04,
Nora's birthday) lands harder.** The episode is a comedy weekend with one knife, and the knife is Jack's, not Lena's.
Theo's nine a.m. ambush with a full tank turns into a **convoy** (the SL; Kevin's mother's Odyssey with Kevin, Margot,
Laney in the Mooney hoodie, and Lena, who "isn't going"); Jack can afford his share of gas (he tells them about the
Anchor — Lena's first she's heard of it). Fork: ride in the SL with Theo or the van with everyone. **Pea Soup Andersen's,
Buellton: the Reliquary tin** (an Altoids tin from the gift shop; the mints poured into Kevin's palm; Saturday's
cigarette sealed in; Sharpie on the lid). Malibu: Station Fire ash on the deck chairs ("Don't touch the deck chairs.
That's the mountains."); the fridge nobody may eat from; the Oscar in the powder room. Lena makes groceries and cooks
(a roux is a vow; Jack stirs while Wes calls on time and she laughs on the deck); **Jack pays Theo his pie** (peach,
Lena's mother's crust) and Theo asks whether God takes IOUs. Rock Band. Sunday: the 8 a.m. at Our Lady of Malibu — James 2
in a Malibu church — Lena coaching Laney through her first Mass in whispers; Theo outside with coffees ("a supporter, not
a member"); Nonna on the phone about First Friday. Fork: **the Getty Villa with Lena (and Margot, who out-Greeks her)**
or the pool with Theo, Laney and Kevin (Poppy on the first ring). **Richard Vance's six minutes** (Margot times him:
6:14): magnetic and awful; mistakes Jack at the sink for help and tips him a hundred. **The labor duel** on the deck that
night, Margot moderating: the just wage, the right of association (*Rerum Novarum*; *Caritas in Veritate* §25), the UAW,
the bailout, *Laborem Exercens* ("work is for man") — and Frank, laid off since June, said aloud to her for the first
time. **She wins it** (2–1). Monday, Labor Day, 7:12 a.m.: *"This is a prepaid call from — Danny — an inmate at Trumbull
Correctional Institution."* Theo in the kitchen; Danny funny and gentle and asking what the ocean looks like; Theo
handles it beautifully and keeps it. Then Lena on the beach stairs, the tin's first deposit, and a cold swim she dares
him into without knowing why he needed it. The drive home: Obama at the AFL-CIO picnic in Cincinnati on the radio;
Gina: dad's in the garage with the radio. Tag: Theo, in the dark: "What's he reading?"

**E04 · "Nativity"** · Tue Sep 8 – Sat Sep 12 · Nativity of Mary (Sept 8) — 🔒 Nora's birthday. *(Re-planned after S1E03
for the played path and the player's notes — the harder turn after the breather; ensemble setpieces as punctuation, not
every week; **a long Jack/Lena two-hander as the emotional centerpiece**. Full plan in `outlines/s1e04.md`.)* Row M empty
(PHIL 1, Hale on Epicurus); the 12:10 Nativity Mass (Osei on the genealogy: "God is not ashamed of his family"); "Happy
birthday to her." / "To who?"; Fitch's white rose. Jack's first shift beside Rudy since Danny's voice (the every-other-Sunday
rhyme lands: "Whoever you got — write him"); the Anchor's kitchen goes dark Thursday (annual hood cleaning: a lost shift, a
free night); **Hale drunk in the back booth, Housman, "Go home, Mr. Costigan"** (🔒). Priya's cupcake: Nora becomes a person.
Wednesday: Hale's clean jacket; Locke and Reid's brave officer; the paper swap ("(Costigan)"; his paper "That God Is the One
Who Pays"); **"You lie!"** in the 4th-floor lounge (a room scene, not a setpiece) — Margot's whiteboard debuts; Jack hears the
racial note for the first time (brief's long arc); Lena declines the fight ("I'm too tired to argue with a whiteboard");
Laney hands her to Jack for the week. Fork: the Point at sunset (the cross, the jasmine, **Julian**) or iftar with Yusuf
(Tariq in Helmand). Thursday: Evan's "PERSONAL"; **Café Bastille — "Fresno"; Lena leaves in the second chorus. THE
TWO-HANDER** (~4,400 words, five scenes, nobody else): the end of the harbor pier; No. 3 (she smokes half); Hopkins' "Thou
art indeed just, Lord" with *Show Lena* in his margin; **her grandmother, the storm, the medal in a plastic bag, the mother
she has never asked**; *Who are you paying for, Costigan?*; **Danny, if the player tells her**; Ocean Road in Danny's jacket
("Nobody yells at an empty room"); counting freight cars on the Delgado steps; "Thank you, Jack"; "Goodnight, Magdalena"
(gated). Friday 9/11: Theo drives Jack to Driftwood to ship the Waugh to Trumbull (the replacement Everyman, "Not on the tab";
Richard's four days in a Lincoln from Toronto, 2001; "I'm good at not asking"). Saturday: the box or the sprinkler (Nonna's
"Go next week"). Tag: Jack's first Saturday shift; olallieberry 9/10; Lena brings the jacket back; Gloria rates her "Pecan";
**Wes calls at 2:04 a.m. Central from a party** — "He sounded happy." Lena's resolve 8 → 5 (4 at the song). No duel.

**E05 · "Moral Luck"** · Sun Sep 13 – Fri Sep 18, 2:10 a.m. · 24th Sunday OT (Is 50; Jas 2:14-18; **Mark 8:27-35**, "Who
do you say that I am?" / "Get behind me, Satan" / "take up his cross"); VMAs (Kanye/Taylor, Sept 13); Exaltation of the Cross
(Mon Sept 14) and Patrick Swayze's death; Our Lady of Sorrows (Tue Sept 15) and the House rebukes Wilson; Baucus's draft
(Wed 16); *Community* premieres (Thu 17); Ramadan's last week. *(Re-planned after S1E04 for the played path and the notes —
no two-hander this week (E06 owes one); **an ensemble setpiece is due**: the Saints opener at Sully's. Full plan in
`outlines/s1e05.md`.)* **Sully's, 10 a.m., black and gold** (Jack owns neither: Margot's black Sleater-Kinney T-shirt or
Theo's closet; Lena on the door with gold beads), the whole core in a booth, Dale the laid-off Lions man from Warren,
*Michigan*, the Browns dying on the corner TV (Favre's Vikings), **Brees's six touchdowns**, Lena painting a gold fleur-de-lis
on Jack's cheekbone at halftime, "Who dat, Costigan. Say it like you mean it," Laney's "Bid Day, sugar. Wear something you
can cry in," Wes texting from the Dome. Sunday 9 p.m. Mass: Osei on Peter, right and wrong in one afternoon; Nonna has
called the rectory to pay for a Mass "in thanksgiving" (Osei can neither confirm nor deny a thing). **Monday: the
moral-luck duel** (Hale's two drivers; Lena the opponent; Hale's "Forgiven by whom?"; Yusuf's diya; "Do you think the
lucky one sleeps?"); the cloister ("You argued that like it was yours" — "It's about me"); **Gina's text: "the convict's
sister"** (shown to Lena or not) and the stairwell call; 3 a.m. *Point Break* with Theo for Swayze. Tuesday: **the money
order to D. Kostelnik, Struthers, Ohio — twenty dollars, the minimum, out of tips; no explanation.** Wednesday: Gauguin;
the paired papers ("That the Lucky Man Owes Nothing" — the thing he least believes); **fork:** 217 after Laney's Pref Night
(Lupe's carnitas, the DRP pillbox, Laney's terror of being chosen) or the Brick with Kevin (the ER, "not a scratch," a scar
telling). Thursday 1 a.m.: **the jacket** ("A girl left it"). Thursday: Evan back from "PERSONAL" (the third driver);
**Pi Lam Bid Day** on Seacliff (Laney runs home in a Pi Lam Rodeo shirt; Margot's *Tide* piece is on the stands); on the
bluff bench, **a prepaid call from Trumbull with Lena beside him** — "Hi, Danny" (path) — cut off by the system mid-sentence:
"Tell Gina — ." Tag: the 2:10 freight on the Delgado steps; Lena texts a number. Lena's resolve 5 → 7.

**E06 · "Welcome"** · Fri Sep 18 – Sun Sep 20 (tag Sun ~11:50 p.m.) · 25th Sunday OT Year B (Wis 2:12, 17-20; Ps 54;
**Jas 3:16–4:3**, "you covet but do not possess"; **Mark 9:30-37**: the argument on the road about who is greatest, silence in
the house, "If anyone wishes to be first, he shall be the last of all and the servant of all," the child — "whoever receives one
such child in my name, receives me"); Rosh Hashanah from sundown Friday; Eid al-Fitr Sunday; Ohio State 38–0 Toledo in
Cleveland (Sat); **Saints W 48–22 at Philadelphia** (Sun, 10 a.m. PT; the glass goes 2–0); Browns L 6–27 at Denver. *(Re-planned
after S1E05 for the played path and the notes — full plan in `outlines/s1e06.md`. **The standing-rule extended two-hander is
owed and is the episode's heart:** "Walk me home, Costigan. The long way." Player loved Lena, Theo and Laney; Margot and Hale;
Danny and Gina — keep investing in Jack's family. No duel; no setpiece: the chastity talk, the cleanup and the BBQ are mid-size
rooms.)* **Cold open:** fire alarm 2, Fri 3:12 a.m., fifty-eight minutes after "Goodnight, Jack"; Laney in the pink Rodeo hat
("They were *lucky*, fireman"); Margot opens an alarm log ("Nineteen days. That's not a schedule. That's a person."); Doug
barefoot at the wrong door again (no comment); **Theo carries out both bags off the top shelf and no espresso machine** ("It's
insured" — 🔒 one sentence); Lena, who has seen Becca's wall post: **"It's a beach cleanup, Jack. It is not a date. Bring
gloves."** **Friday:** Hale's office hours (Hollis 314, by email summons) — Hale lends Jack his own Oxford copy of Williams's
*Moral Luck* ("Bring it back with your margins in it"), asks how an eighteen-year-old knows the lucky one doesn't sleep; Jack
may tell him about Danny (gated; 🔒 the S3 footnote); "Miss Broussard was here at two." **Fork:** Theo's cut of the Sully's
footage in 412 ("Look at your face, Costigan") or Margot's last legal cloves on Mission ("I need a pallbearer"). Gina 4:12 p.m.:
"i said it. danny. she looked away first." **Friday night: "Love Is Patient: Our Chastity Story"** (Newman lounge; Peter &
Kenzie's slideshow, Jason Mraz; Joey; Becca's sincere question; Kevin's smile at "your future wife" and Margot watching Kevin —
🔒 E07); **Margot reads Eph 5:22–24 and asks Kenzie whether she plans to submit**; Kenzie's v. 21/v. 25 answer is better than
Margot expected; Peter's "I make the call. Prayerfully." isn't; Jack and Lena look at each other and table it ("Not tonight" —
§24.5); Jack may ask "What do you do when you fall?" → "By God's grace, we haven't had to find out" (🔒). **Saturday:** Becca's
beach cleanup at Campus Point (her team; pink spare gloves; seven-or-nine children; the scar at the waterline — the Pacific; a
Las Olas condom mistaken for a jellyfish and Becca's "I've pulled a calf"; Lena thirty yards down the beach with Joey, laughing;
Jack and Lena trade *Caritas in Veritate* §48–51 across the sand); the BBQ in the courtyard (Osei at the grill; the sprinkler at
grace; Tyler's coffee; Kenzie's "Good question"); **Becca: "I told my mother this was a date. Was it?"** → date #1, retroactively.
5 p.m.: **Danny's first letter on the page** ("Tell Gina it ain't hers to carry. I got it." 🔒; "Tell the veil girl thanks for
asking my name"); the call home (OSU 38–0 in Cleveland — Frank yelled at the TV "in a good way"); **Nonna asks "E la ragazza?"
twice.** The Anchor (Rudy: "You wrote him?" "He wrote back."). **Ocean Road, 1:31 a.m.: "License and registration."** — Ray
Ochoa's bit for his son; "Pop."; the bag he doesn't open (🔒); a bike light from his trunk; Rafi after: "Not bars, though."
**THE TWO-HANDER** (~4,300, Sun 1:52–3:55 a.m.; nobody else after the porch): the Pi Lam barn party (Laney carried upstairs by
her sisters — "Take her home, fireman, she's being a *mother*"; Theo staying to watch her, "I'm up anyway"); **"Walk me home,
Costigan. The long way."**; east, down the bluff stairs below the bench where she said hi to Danny; the dark beach (two nights
past new moon); the 2:10 freight on the bluff above them, which neither counts; **the creek mouth** (her hand on his forearm —
the one restraint sentence; 🔒); the Campus Point lifeguard tower — **Becca** ("because it seemed like the right thing to want"
→ "That's the worst reason I've ever heard, and I've heard you defend the Browns") and **Wes** (missed 8:04, called at 9:40 from
somewhere loud; "he calls from somewhere loud a lot"; **"I've decided not to notice"** — the partying only, 🔒 no Mass); "Don't.
Don't be funny right now. Just stay." (if he jokes); up the Marine Lab stairs and across the dark Commons; **Ochoa** (medal;
subject change — 🔒); **Danny's letter** ("It ain't hers to carry"); Mémère calls her by her aunt's name and she answers to it;
"Why Marisol?" "Money." (medal 🔒) / "You know why I came" (🔒 the card); the Lagoon bench where people go to break up —
**"We'd have to be something first, Costigan."** (end-card tag). **Sunday 6:10 a.m.:** Theo in with wet hems — **"I run now."**
**Fork:** Sully's with Lena and Dale (2–0; "Next week, kid" paid) or Eid with Yusuf (Tariq calls from Helmand). **Sunday 9 p.m.
Mass:** Osei — "On the road they argued about who was the greatest. In the house He asked them what they'd been talking about,
and they were silent"; Laney at her second Mass in her Pi Lam letters under Lena's cardigan. **Tag:** Jack writes Danny back.
Lena's resolve 7 → 6 (the party) → 5 (Wes named) → 7 (Mass). *(Supersedes the earlier E06 notes: the grandmother and the Danny
exchange were spent in E04–E05; Lena already knows Danny and has spoken to him.)*

**E07 · "Cloves"** · Tue Sep 22 – Sun Sep 27 (extended through Sunday so the Saints at Buffalo and the 26th Sunday land in
an episode; E08 opens Tue Sep 29) · FDA flavored-cigarette ban (Tue); the equinox (Tue 2:18 p.m. PDT); Padre Pio (Wed); the
G-20 in Pittsburgh (Thu–Fri; Qom announced Fri); **Ohio State 30–0 Illinois** (Sat); **Saints 27–7 at Buffalo (3–0)**; 26th
Sunday OT B (Num 11:25–29; Jas 5:1–6; **Mark 9:38–48** — "if your hand causes you to sin"). *(Re-planned after S1E06 for the
played path and the notes — full plan in `outlines/s1e07.md`. **Player: lean in on the precipice** — the realization a little
closer, nothing crossed, Wes still there, slow burn; no two-hander owed (E08); Lena central; Becca keeps her blend.)* Cold open:
**Margot's funeral card** under 412's door (this path's first invitation: "Bring pie. The good pie, Costigan."). **Nonna's
Mass** at the 12:10 (Luke 8:19–21, "my mother and my brothers"), Lena beside him; Becca: "Friday. Six. I'll drive." **Gina
friends Lena on Facebook** — and finds the relationship status, and Becca's wall post. The Anchor: Ray at the counter; Gloria's
pie for a funeral. **The wake on the Delgado roof** (ALARM WILL SOUND — *it won't*): **"Dearly beloved. We are gathered here
today to bury a cigarette."**; the eulogy's "my own body" turns into **the 2 a.m. argument** (Margot vs. Lena, Kevin and Jack
at the table) — **"This stopped being a game an hour ago."** — **Anders**; neither concedes; both cry; Lena asks his name. Kevin
walks Margot down (🔒 he comes out to her; Jack misreads them as a couple). **Jack and Lena alone on the roof till sunrise**
(~1,800 words; not a full two-hander): "You argued that like it was yours"; the sentence she didn't finish at the tower — she
still won't; **Wes has bought a ticket for Columbus Day**. Margot's whiteboard: TUESDAY — NO SCORE. (BEREAVEMENT.)
**Wednesday, no sleep: the friendship duel** in Hale's seminar — their papers, "That a Man and a Woman Can Be Friends" (Jack)
and "That They Can't" (Lena); Lena favored; Hale: one of them has proved the other's thesis. Hale's Housman for Danny (gated).
Tyler's coffee. Thursday: Kevin or Margot (the misread); section (Evan, Kierkegaard); Lena keeps his paper: "Sunday."
**Friday: Becca date #2** — the Fishbowl (Laney: "Is she Amish or just punctual?"; Lena: "Laney. Be nice."; Theo flawless,
texting; Kevin: "Leave it alone.") — Marisol High football in her brother's Ranger; the First Way on the bleachers; a kiss on
the cheek "so there's no confusion." Saturday: the call home ("Who's Idaho?" — "And the veil girl?"). Sunday fork: Sully's with
Lena and Dale (3–0) or the 9:30 and Coffee & Donuts with Becca; Osei on the hand. **Tag: her red ink, under the door —
"Defense."** Lena's resolve 7 → 8 → 6 → **5** → 6. No danger window. Duel record 3–1 → 3–2 on the default (Lena) result.

**E08 · "The Little Way"** · Tue Sep 29 – Sun Oct 4 · Michaelmas (Tue; Jack's confirmation saint); St. Jerome (Wed); **St.
Thérèse (Thu Oct 1; Lena's middle name)**; Guardian Angels (Fri); the Blessing of the Animals (Sat Oct 3, a day before Francis);
27th Sunday B (Gen 2:18 "It is not good for the man to be alone"; Mark 10:2–16); the Samoa tsunami and the Padang quake on the
Fishbowl TV; Chicago loses the Olympics (Fri); OSU 33–14 at Indiana; **Saints 24–10 Jets (4–0)**; Browns lose in OT (0–4).
*(Re-planned after S1E07 — retitled from "Michaelmas"; full plan in `outlines/s1e08.md`. **The two-hander is the player's own
design** (brief, "S1E08 two-hander") and the episode is built around it.)* Since "Defense." they have been **scrupulously just
friends** — Monday's seminar ("Good afternoon, Jack"), Tuesday's Michaelmas 12:10 ("Happy feast day, Jack"; "We're fine, Jack"),
Wednesday's seminar on lying, where they agree with each other all hour and Hale is appalled. Tuesday: **Danny's Michaelmas
letter** ("Happy St. Mike's, Professor… Tell Lena hey… Don't feel bad about it… That's an order."); Theo: **"Does he catch
anybody? Or just throw?"** (🔒). Wednesday night, **THE TWO-HANDER** (~4,500; the Romance chunk): the Delgado laundry room at
ten — Jack erasing the Hopkins for Trumbull; Lena with two baskets; awkward, painful, polite ("How's Becca?" "Great." "How's
Wes?" "Great." "Nine days."); **she erases *Show Lena* herself** ("Friends help."). Midnight is her feast: he gives her a used
*Story of a Soul* with his pencil margins in it — **"You wrote in it. You *wrote in it*, Costigan."** — he reaches for the eraser;
"Don't you dare." **The crack** (a third of the way into the night): an unofficial duel on the Little Way, no referee ("Easy for
you." / the table of sinners / *empty hands*); Lupe's at one ("Para los novios." "We're friends." "Claro."); **Shipwreck Cove under
a gibbous moon going down into the Pacific** — Thérèse read aloud by moonlight, a Cajun two-step on the hard sand (or not),
**"Do you know what you're doing yet?"** — he starts, can't, admits he doesn't know, and says Becca is so nice, so sweet; **Lena
laughs before she can stop it** ("Come up with a better answer before somebody else asks you, Youngstown. That is the worst thing
you can say about a person in this situation.") **and can't hide her relief; he sees it.** At two, Becca is praying for him (her
promise). They walk back guilty; at the Delgado elevator, ~4 a.m.: **"That was a terrible thing to be glad about."** Laney, 4:41:
she came in and went straight to her knees, "not asking. (asking)"; Theo: "Did it come clean?" **From the guilt:** Thursday Jack
ships the Hopkins and Hale's Housman, stands behind Lena at the UCen counter while she argues a jar of the Pacific into the mail
for Wes, and from the east stairwell **asks Becca out for Friday — he plans it, he pays** (Friday sauce / elephant seals /
horses); Gloria rates Idaho sight unseen ("Apple. That's the one you bring home to your mother.") and asks after the pecan.
Friday, **date #3: Becca thrilled** ("Nobody's ever planned a thing for me"; "I've been praying for two in the morning"). **Lena
turns to Wes** (calls she makes; the stairwell laugh; the jar; Wes thrilled). Saturday, **the Blessing of the Animals** (the
ensemble setpiece): Thomas the crab ("Who brings a *crab*."); Hale and Bishop Berkeley ("He's deaf, Father. You'll have to bless
him louder."); Osei's hand on the dog's ribs; Berkeley and Knox's limerick; **neither resolve takes — the freeze lasts about five
minutes** and Jack and Lena are laughing together in front of Becca; Becca's question makes Osei laugh for a full minute; Mrs.
Fitch's "you two"; Margot's trial faith column; Jack's last Kevin-and-Margot joke, shut down, and he stops. Call home ("E
l'altra?"); optional confession (Lena coming out of the coat closet as he goes in). Sunday fork: Sully's (Sharper's 99 yards; the
bell; Wes from the Dome, "TELL THE OHIO GUY WHO DAT") or the 9:30 with Becca (Lena's play-by-play texts under the table). Osei on
Genesis 2: the first thing in the Bible that is not good is loneliness. Tag: the full moon in the six-inch window, Lena's laugh
on the stairs ("Five days"), Becca's "Friday was the best night." Lena's resolve 6 → 7 → 6 → 5 → **4** (the cove) → 5 → 7
(confession, Sat) → **6**. No danger window (Lena never drops below 4; both must be ≤3).

**E09 · "Landfall"** · Fri Oct 9 – Mon Oct 12 · *(retitled from "Columbus")* · Obama's Nobel Peace Prize (Fri; announced before
dawn Pacific); Leif Erikson Day (Fri — Margot observes it instead); OSU–Wisconsin (Sat; Researcher); **Saints bye**; 28th Sunday OT B
(**Mark 10:17–30**, the rich young man — "Jesus, looking at him, loved him"; "Go, sell what you have"; the camel; "with persecutions")
and **Damien of Molokai canonized** (Sun); **Browns 6–3 at Buffalo, their first win** (Sun; Researcher verify); Columbus Day (Mon; no
mail). *(Re-planned after S1E08 for the played path and the player's notes — full plan in `outlines/s1e09.md`. **The ensemble setpiece
is the weekend itself:** Wes in person for the first time; Jack brings Becca to everything; Theo and Laney work overtime and are truly
awful (comedy, not cruelty); Wes is a good guy who hits it off with Jack and, as the weekend goes on, plainly dislikes what he sees
between Jack and Lena; Becca doesn't notice and trusts Jack wholeheartedly. Opens with Jack and Becca alone. A little more on the
strange threads.)* **Cold open: Friday 6 a.m., Becca's Ranger and the Marine Lab touch tank** (her work-study shift; Thomas home; the
Nobel on KCBX "before anybody's had coffee"; she mailed Rosie a birthday card with Thomas on it). Friday: the Fishbowl Nobel; Margot's
LEIF ERIKSON DAY whiteboard and one sentence about Lena at Thursday's "Fresno" (left in the second chorus — "Again."); **Jack asks
Laney for an idea for Becca** (brief running bit) — a poem, out loud, about her eyes, rhymed, "and then, sugar, one knee" — and can't
say what color Becca's eyes are. The Odyssey's flat (Theo, unacknowledged); Theo fetches Wes from SBA alone in the two-seat SL with a
calligraphy sign; Jack and Kevin change the tire while Lena waits on the curb (Danny's "hey" delivered, `e8_danny=="told"`); at 6:20
the SL comes in — Wes with the jar, empty ("I drank it"): **"You're the Ohio guy."** Theo has cancelled Wes's motel ("bedbugs"): Wes
sleeps on an air mattress in 412. Friday night: **Laney's Friday bonfire at Shipwreck Cove** — Wes and Jack hit it off; Wes waltzes Lena
to "Jolie Blonde" by the ribs (the song she taught Jack there eight nights ago — on this save); Becca asks where Jack learned to waltz;
**the poem** (path: on one knee — the cove thinks it's a proposal; Becca: "Not yet, Jack Costigan."). Saturday: **Theo's "Columbus Day
Expedition" to the Madonna Inn** (Meridian letterhead; Becca drives the SL; Laney's van seating chart) and **Laney's Newlywed Game** in a
pink booth over champagne cake — Becca knows all Jack's answers; Jack doesn't know hers; Laney, in Lena's cardigan, finds **the
Reliquary**; Wes reads "partially consumed by the Reliquarian" and laughs a beat late. Five o'clock: Wes wants to see the old light;
**Lena won't walk past the Lighthouse** ("I'm cold" — medal) and Wes looks at Jack. **Fork:** walk Lena back / go on to the Point with
Wes and Becca (Jack's first sight of the cross: NORA in the crossbar; last week's flowers gone brown on kitchen string; Becca prays;
Wes: "She's scared of something up here"). The Anchor: **Gloria meets Becca ("Apple. Told you, hon.") and rates Wes on sight:
"Peach."**; a fisherman's Nobel crack with a racial note, and Jack hears the difference between his own "For what?" and that (#47).
**2 a.m., Campus Point, the lifeguard tower, Theo's Jameson:** Wes in the water to his knees (the scar: "I walked into the Pacific
Ocean," path); Theo asked him in the car whether you ever get one you can't reach; **the attic, told as healed** ("I couldn't kneel for
a year after. Then she came along and I could"); Jack may give him Danny in exchange; **Mémère's ring on the flip phone** (Odile gave it
to him at Easter, "for when"; he'll ask Tony after she graduates); "Don't say nothing to her"; **"Take care of her. She thinks she's the
one doing the carrying."** 🔒 Half a lie, told to himself. Dawn: **Theo's SL turns in off Ocean Road from the west, sand on the mats —
"I drive to where I run"** (🔒); Lena on the Delgado steps since five in Wes's Tulane sweatshirt, watching them come up the walk with
Wes's arm across Jack's shoulders. Sunday: Lena on the stairs — "What did y'all talk about?" (Jack keeps Wes's confidence on every
path). **Fork:** the 9:30 and Coffee & Donuts with Becca, Lena and Wes (no Saints; no excuse) — Osei on Damien ("He did not cure
anyone. He stayed."), Wes kneeling beside Lena (🔒), Mrs. Fitch's "you two" twice — or **Sully's for the Browns' first win** with Wes
(who needs a team) and Dale; Frank calls ("Two completions. I counted. Good."); Gina: Becca's card made Rosie's birthday. **Sunday
night: red beans in the Delgado kitchen — "Stir. Don't stop."** Lena holds the spoon out behind her without looking; Wes: "Same as the
video" (Laney's Malibu roux, on Facebook since September); **the duel at the stove — "On the Rich Young Man"** (the roux goes peanut
butter → penny → Hershey bar across three moves); Becca, to Wes, overheard: "I love that he has somebody to argue with. I'm no good at
it."; **Lena takes the spoon back and gives it to Becca** ("Becca. Come learn. He only knows how to stir." — the week's one small
generous devastating gesture). Monday: the Anacapa lot at 8:20 — Becca's banana bread for the plane; "Take care of yourself, Becca";
**Wes's handshake for Jack, not the hug: "See you, Ohio."**; the 4–0 pulls out. Hale's email (papers: "That a Man Can Know His Own Heart"
/ "That He Cannot," Wednesday). **Tag:** the landing, 8:04 p.m.; Becca prayed for Wes's flight; two floors down, Lena on the phone to New
Orleans: **"He's my friend, Wes."** Lena's resolve 6 → 7 → 6 → 5 → 7 → 6 → **5**. No danger window. The retreat that never takes (#66) is
suspended (nothing to retreat from with Wes in the room); guilty fun (#67) turns into generosity, and Wes's trust is built on it.

**E10 · "I Call the Living"** · Tue Oct 13 – Sat Oct 17 · *(retitled from "Midterms"; the English of the Ferrer Tower's *Vivos voco*)* · the
first big storm of the season (Tue; Typhoon Melor's remnants — 50-mph gusts, 1–1.5 in/hr on the coast [V]); Dow 10,000 (Wed); St. Teresa of
Ávila, Balloon Boy and **Obama in New Orleans** (Thu); *Where the Wild Things Are* (Fri); Purdue 26, Ohio State 18, Diwali and the new moon
(Sat). Saints 4–0, idle till Sunday's unbeaten Giants (off-page). *(Re-planned after S1E09 for the played path and **the player's own two-hander
design** (brief, "S1E10 two-hander") — full plan in `outlines/s1e10.md`.)* **Cold open:** fire alarm 3, Tue ~3:20 a.m., in the storm (Margot:
"Twenty-five."; Doug through a puddle asleep; Theo's two bags 🔒); Lena alone at the far end of the overhang in **Wes's green Tulane
sweatshirt**, "Morning, Jack."; Lena and Laney not speaking (the tin) — **Laney claims asylum in 412** ("Granted."). Tuesday: **Danny's letter**
("I don't wish. I decided." 🔒); the second money order; the Anchor in the gale (Gloria: "Lots of things work, hon."; Dulce and Rosario — Lena's
Sunday soup, Gideon seed); **Priya unlocks the flooded RA storage closet — Nora's box, the journal's first page; the box goes to 401**; Jack's
thesis at 3 a.m.: "A man knows his own heart by what he says yes to." Wednesday: the war council in 412 ("For Halloween, sugar —" "No."); **Hale's
seminar** — no duel (midterm week; papers swapped, corpses Monday): "Good afternoon, Mr. Costigan." / "Mr. Costigan has been demoted." /
Jeremiah 17:10, "you have been asked to steal His job"; Lena's last line: *A woman who says she knows her own heart is either a saint or lying*;
the Brick's eighth floor (24 hours, midterm week): **Becca's MIDTERM SURVIVAL bag and a clementine for Lena**; Jack's margin: *Which are you?*
**THE TWO-HANDER (the Romance chunk, ~4,800; Wed night → Thu 7:10 a.m.)** in the player's three movements: **raw and sideways** — 4:00 a.m., the
eighth floor, her margin *Then what did you tell him?* beside his thesis; the duel **`cor`, "On the Heart"** (unofficial; Paul's "not thereby
acquitted"; "Wes told me" — he asked you to take care of me and you said yes with a *bottle*; "Y'all don't get to *assign* me"; **"It took me a
pier"**; "You're honest *at* people"; Jack's Personal option — Seacliff; HEART: **"Then what am I?"** unanswered); **five minutes of
pretending** (the elevator; "We're fine."); **the reckless night** — the Ferrer Tower's storm-warped door under caution tape (**"Don't." "I
wasn't going to."**), a hundred and sixty steps, the Ephesians bit on the stairs, **VIVOS VOCO · MORTUOS PLANGO · FULGURA FRANGO**, her back to
the west 🔒, Reliquary No. 5 (path), "Who do you think plays the piano in the basement?", and **at 5:00 a.m. Lena plays "When the Saints Go
Marching In" on the carillon** with her fists, one bell flat ("That's not flat. That's *New Orleans*."); Theo: "Costigan. Is that the Saints.";
a campus cruiser; **hide behind the great bell / run for the Brick** (Priya: "I didn't see you."); the same table at dawn — **Aquinas and
football** ("that's the dream girl right there"; "it was nothing"), "He says he out-waits me" (the ring, unsaid), **restraint scene #1** (her
head on his shoulder; an hour; his arm), WES at 6:02, Becca's "prayed for u at 2 like always" at 6:41, **the 7:00 chimes and the near-miss**
(who holds is the player's; no kiss); "Midterm, Youngstown." / "Sit somewhere else." Thursday: Hale's midterm ("the first thing that tower has
done that I've enjoyed") and **the realization** (the First Way question: he described Lena); **fork:** the 12:10 with Becca (Teresa; Lena on
Catherine) / the Fishbowl (Balloon Boy; Lena reads the crawl — Obama in her city); the boy found in the attic (Jack: Pauger Street).
**Friday 1:25 a.m., the steps: Laney, Big/Little (Sloane), Sloane's Point-wall lore 🔒, Kaylee; Lena comes down — "I've got her, Jack."**
Friday: Margot's suspects (1. A CATHOLIC 2. A DIFFERENT CATHOLIC 3. THE HOLY GHOST); ***Wild Things*** with Becca ("and it was still hot"),
Maria's note, "Not yet," the costume list, **"You look at me like I'm homework." / "Then do your homework, Jack. I'll wait." / "Due
Halloween."** Saturday: Theo's **SAINTS & SINNERS** invitation; "Purdue."; **Nonna: "Danny?"** 🔒; Priya's diya on the box. **Tag:** the six o'clock
chime, a new padlock on the tower, Lena on the Brick's steps holding up five fingers — "Tomorrow." Lena's resolve 5 → 4 (the Brick) → 4 (the
night) → 5 (Thu) → **6** (the steps). Jack 8 → ends ~6–9. No danger window. **Not this week:** the Delgado roof (#65), postcards (E14), the cross.
*(E10 played as planned on this path — §21 status after S1E10: the HEART verdict without the stranger ("Then we're both lying, some days."); "Magdalena" at
seven; the Fishbowl fork; Lena ends 5.)*

**E11 · "Lucia"** · Tue Oct 20 – Sun Oct 25 · *(retitled from "Big Sur"; the hamlet below the Hermitage where the van gives out — and Lucy, patron of
eyes, on the Sunday of blind Bartimaeus)* · the Anglican announcement (Tue Oct 20); the NLCS (Rollins's walk-off Mon; the Phillies clinch Wed); the
Orionids; H1N1 a national emergency (Sat); **30th Sunday OT B (Mark 10:46–52, "Master, I want to see")**; **Saints 46–34 at Miami (6–0)** after trailing
big; Browns 3–31 at Green Bay (Researcher). *(Re-planned after S1E10 for the played path and the **player's reading** — both relationships on borrowed time;
Jack pushes through anyway because of the ring; full plan in `outlines/s1e11.md`. **Halloween stays E12** — E10's `nextTime` already promised this episode
the road trip. No two-hander owed; Laney at the same dose.)* **The deliberate retreat:** since the bells Jack is careful (the ring; never spoken) and so,
after "Magdalena," is Lena; the player practices it (the glass, the seat, the tin that starves, the split at Big Sur village) against the alliance, a
one-heater motel, a monk and the Saints. **Cold open:** Lena writes 5–0 on the Odyssey glass; Theo's **BIG SUR EXPEDITION** letterhead; he has already gotten
Jack's Saturday covered (Gloria: "Baked Alaska"); Kerouac's *Big Sur* as required reading. Tue: the Anglicans in the Fishbowl (Margot's best argument;
Lena: "You don't come home because you're mad at the neighbors"). **Wed: the duel `exit` — "On Leaving"** (Hale referees; official): Jack EXIT (Newman;
Dorothy Day leaving Forster), Lena LOYALTY (John 6:68; Royce; Ruth; "you stay and you make it tell the truth"); Yusuf (Abdul Rahman, 2006), Caleb ("Nobody
built me a structure"); 🔒 Hale's Cranmer line; "When you leave something, look at it." Thu: the 12:10 seat kept (Osei on Luke 12); **Becca can't come**
(the tank weekend; the swine flu) — "Bring me a fruitcake… the only honest fruitcake in America"; Evan's Kierkegaard (🔒). **Sat: six people, one minivan,
Highway 1** — Osei blesses the Odyssey; Priya's "a lot of points" (🔒); the seat (RESERVED); Margot's Ferrer interrogation (the alibi in unison); O'Connor's
peafowl read aloud (🔒 E12); "Two Catholic boys named Jack"; the starving tin; Gorda; Bixby (Theo back from the rail 🔒); Nepenthe (Welles and Hayworth;
the alliance's costume intel — "a *bird*"); **fork:** the Henry Miller Library with Theo and Margot (her Sunday question 🔒) / Pfeiffer Beach with Lena,
Laney and Kevin (**the Pacific telling, in the water, to Laney**); **the vote** (Vespers at the Hermitage / sunset at McWay Falls); the overheat near Lucia;
**the Santa Lucia Motor Court** (Verna; one heater); Kevin and Theo at the room chart (🔒); **the payphone at 8:04** (Kevin's quarters; "I love you" and the
